CORPORATE PROFILE
公司簡介
China Modern Dairy Holdings Limited (the “Company” or “Modern
Dairy” together with its subsidiaries, the “Group”) is currently the
largest dairy farming operator and fresh raw milk producer in the
People’s Republic of China (the “PRC”) in terms of its herd size and
milk yield. Modern Dairy operates 26 dairy farms in the PRC with
dairy cows over 233 thousand heads and annual milk yield over 1.39
million tons. The Company initiated the production model of “Zero-
distance Integration of Forage Grass Planting, Cows Breeding and Milk
Processing within Two Hours”, which is the first and the only enterprise
at present in the PRC that applies integrated production model with
SGS certification. Modern Dairy’s products have been awarded the
Gold Prize of Monde Selection which is generally regarded as the
“Nobel Prize” in the food industry, for the past six consecutive years.


 中國現代牧業控股有限公司（  「本公司」或「現代牧業」連同其附屬公司
（
「本集團」 ），按牛群數量及原奶供應量，現為中華人民共和國（   「中國」）
 最大的乳牛畜牧營運商及最大的原料奶生產商。現代牧業於中國經營
 26個乳牛牧場，擁有超過23.3萬頭乳牛及每年產奶量超過1.39百萬噸。
 本集團首創「種養加一體化、零距離2小時」的生產模式，是中國首家、
 也是目前唯一一家一體化生產模式並通過SGS認證的企業。現代牧業
 的產品於過去六年連續在素有食品業「諾貝爾獎」之稱的世界食品品質
 評鑒大會中獲得金獎。

                                                                                   CEO’S STATEMENT
                                                                                          總裁報告書



After several years of industry adjustment, China’s cow herd size        經過幾年的行業出清，中國奶牛存欄下降，奶價
has decreased and milk price has made a significant recovery. In         回升明顯。在供給端，奶牛養殖逐步升級為以規
terms of supply, the upstream dairy is gradually being upgraded to       模養殖為主導，小型牧場及家庭作坊式牧場陸續
large-scale dairy farming. Small-scale farms and family-owned farms      退出。奶牛養殖行業的週期性減弱，未來原奶將
are gradually phased out. As cyclical nature of the dairy farming        主要由規模牧場生產提供。規模養殖的行業壁壘
industry weakens, raw milk will be provided mainly by large-scale        深厚，我們將更充分享受週期向上的紅利。在需
farms in the future. With the high industry entry barriers to large-     求端，展望未來三年，一線城市的消費升級和低
scale dairy farming, we will fully enjoy the advantage of rising raw     線城市消費增長將持續支持奶製品需求的穩健增
milk cycle. In terms of demand, looking forward to the next three        長，而高品質的原奶作為常溫奶、低溫巴氏奶等
years, consumption upgrades in first-tier cities and consumption         產品的原料，需求量更是巨大。
growth in low-tier cities will continue to support the steady growth
of demand for dairy products. The demand for high-quality raw
milk is even greater as it is used as raw materials for UHT milk, low-
temperature pasteurized milk and other products.

Looking ahead to 2020, the original aspiration remains unchanged         展望2020，困難雖在，惟初心不改。2020年，我
despite of the difficulties. 2020 will be the 15 th year since the       們將邁入成立的第15年、上市的第10年。我們堅
Company was established and the 10th year since we were listed           持立足自身，不斷提升單產、降低成本，提高運
on the Hong Kong Stock Exchange. We insist on being self-reliant,        營效率；面向市場，則把握週期趨勢，緊貼市場
continuously improving yields, reducing costs and improving              發展，適度增群擴產，提升集團的市場競爭力。
operational efficiency; facing the market, we grasp the cyclical         作為中國最大的乳牛畜牧公司及最大的原奶生產

 Heat Stress Alleviation Measures                                         緩解熱應激措施
 Heat stress as a result of high temperature in summer has become         由於夏季高溫所帶來的熱應激對乳牛的影響已成
 a seasonal problem for milkable cows. The yield of each milkable         為一個季節性問題，每頭成乳牛的產量一般會
 cow is generally slightly reduced due to the impact caused by            受熱應激影響略有下降。為緩解熱應激帶來的影
 heat stress. To minimize the impact, the Group has implemented           響，本集團已採取緩解計劃。本集團引進高效通
 alleviation plans. The Group equipped cowshed with efficient             風系統設備裝備牛舍，如強力的風扇，升級牛舍
 ventilation system, such as powerful fans, upgraded the facilities in    設施，加強每頭奶牛的健康水平。
 barns and enhanced the health of each dairy cow.

 to enable the Company to appropriately increase its herd size,          規模，保持合理的牛群結構，保持原料奶產量穩
 maintain reasonable cow herd structure and maintain a stable            定增長；(ii)加強新希望與本公司之間的合作，以
 growth of raw milk yield; (ii) strengthen the cooperation between       保持本公司原料奶穩定的客戶基礎；(iii)減少本公
 New Hope and the Company so as to maintain a stable customer            司負債並控制本公司的財務風險；(iv)實現質押股
 base of the raw milk of the Company; (iii) decrease the amount          份的價值。
 of liabilities borne by the Company and control the Company’s
 financial risks; and (iv) realize the value of the pledged Collateral
 Shares.

 2019. For details, please refer to the Company’s announcement           閱本公司日期為2019年7月18日及9月20日的公
 dated 18 July and 20 September 2019.                                    告。

 SOCIAL RESPONSIBILITY                                                                                社會責任
 The Group proactively fulfills its social responsibility, adhering                                   本集團積極履行社會責任，秉持安全質量為先的
 to the principle that safety and quality come first, and actively                                    原則，積極開發及實施現代化的科學養殖及餵
 develops and implements modern scientific breeding and feeding                                       飼技術。努力在發展業務與社會責任之間取得平
 know-how. The Group puts great effort to strike a balance between                                    衡，讓廣大持份者感受到現代牧業傳遞的關愛和
 business growth and social responsibility, so as to convey the                                       幸福。
 Group’s care and blessing to all stakeholders.

 The COVID-19 erupted in China in January 2020. As China’s largest                                    2020年1月，國內爆發新型冠狀病毒肺炎。作為
 raw milk producer, The Company concerns about the spread of                                          國內最大的原奶生產商，本公司緊密關注疫情發
 the pandemic and proactively takes up our social responsibility.                                     展，積極承擔社會責任。疫情期間，本公司的原
 During the pandemic, the Company maintained normal production                                        奶生產銷售均保持正常，保證社會的牛奶消費需
 and sales, thereby guaranteeing a supply that meets consumer                                         求。本公司心繫一線，向疫情一線地區捐贈價值
 needs. The Company also paid great attention to the frontline,                                       高達總計人民幣1,000萬元的「現代牧業」品牌系列
 and donated “Modern Dairy” branded dairy products worth up to                                        奶類產品，支援醫院、疾控中心、治安崗亭等防
 a total value of RMB10 million in order to assist frontline facilities                               疫一線場所。本公司將生產安全放在首位，對生
 such as local hospitals, disease prevention and control centers and                                  產區加強消毒，對員工身體狀況持續監測，利用
 public security posts. The Company prioritizes safe production by                                    遠程軟件靈活辦公，關注員工健康，嚴格防疫絕
 sterilizing production zones and pays great attention to employee                                    不鬆懈。
 health by continuously monitoring the health of our employees and
 adopting remote online working procedures. The Company also
 implemented rigorous disease prevention and control measures.

 T o b e t t e r i l l u s t r a t e t h e G r o u p’s s o c i a l r e s p o n s i b i l i t i e s,   為更能說明本集團的社會責任、環境保護以及社

PROSPECTS                                                               展望
In the past two years, favorable to dairy industry, the central         過去兩年，中央政府支持奶業發展，扶植本土奶
government fostered the development of local dairy industry by          業，陸續出台多項政策加強奶業扶持。2020年2
successively introducing a number of policies to strengthen dairy       月，中央一號文件強調保障重要農產品有效供
industry support. In February 2020, the No. 1 Central Document          給，支持奶業、禽類、牛羊等生產，引導優化肉
stressed the need to ensure the effective supply of important           類消費結構。
agricultural products, support the production of dairy, poultry,
cattle and sheep, and guide the optimization of meat consumption
structure.

Currently, raw milk market is short in supply and it is expected that   目前原奶市場供不應求，預計原奶緊缺將持續。
the shortage of raw milk will continue. With the withdrawal of small-   隨着中小牧場退出，奶牛養殖行業的週期性減
and medium-sized dairy farms from the market, the cyclicity of the      弱，未來原奶將主要由規模牧場生產提供。而規
dairy farming industry has weakened, and raw milk will be mainly        模養殖的行業壁壘高，本公司將更充分享受行業
provided by large-scale dairy farms in the future. The industry entry   週期向上的紅利，充分發揮行業龍頭的優勢。
barriers of large-scale farming are high, and the Company will fully
enjoy the benefits of the industry cycle and the advantages as
industry leader.

In 2020, the Company enters its 15th year of establishment and 10th     2020年，本公司將邁入成立的第15年、上市的
year of listing. In the new dairy industry cycle, the Company adheres   第10年。在嶄新的奶業週期，本公司堅持立足自
to its own base, continuously improves unit production, reduces         身，不斷提升單產、降低成本，提高運營效率；
costs and improves operational efficiency. Facing the market, it        面向市場，則把握週期趨勢，緊貼市場發展，適
grasps the cyclical trend, keeps abreast of market developments,        度增群擴產，提升集團的市場競爭力。本公司將
moderately increases its production and expansion and improves          繼續加強上下游的協同整合，打造原奶生產全鏈
the Group’s market competitiveness. The Company will continue           條佈局，增強國內原奶生產第一品牌的市場競爭
to strengthen the coordination and integration of upstream and          力，為股東創造更大的價值，為消費者提供富營
downstream, create a full-chain layout of raw milk production,          養價值的原奶，為中國奶業升級轉型做出不懈的
enhance the market competitiveness of the first domestic brand          貢獻。
of raw milk production, create greater value for shareholders,
and provide consumers with nutritious raw milk, so as to make
unremitting contributions to the upgrading and transformation of

        evaluate the risk portfolio accordingly.                               估風險組合。

The Company has in place an internal control system. The              本公司設有內部監控系統。該系統框架使本集團
framework of the system enables the Group to achieve objectives       能夠實現有關運營有效性及效率、財務報告可靠

     objectives, forming a basis for determining how risks should         何管理風險提供基礎。

 Employee Partnership Program                                          員工合夥人計畫
 As the Company’s business scale continues to expand, operating        隨著本公司業務規模的不斷擴展，經營管理及激
 management and incentive system shall keep up with the times.         勵制度需要與時俱進，傳統的「制度管理+目標實
 The traditional rationale of management by objectives has             現」的管理有其局限性。因此，如何激發員工積極
 limitations. How to ignite employees’ working enthusiasm to serve     服務公司、增強員工主人翁意識、與公司共同發
 the company proactively, and enhance their sense of ownership         展，是公司需要深入考慮的。為此，本公司在現
 and strive for the development of the company together are the        有限股獎勵計畫外，特別制定了員工合夥人盈利
 topics that the Company needs to cope with in depth. Therefore,       分享激勵計畫（「員工合夥人計畫」
                                                                                      ），以打造員工
 on top of the existing restricted share award schemes, the            與公司的利益共同體。
 Company has specially formulated an employee partnership profit-
 sharing program (the “Employee Partnership Program”) with aim
 to build a community of shared interests amongst the Company
 and its employees.

 Many leading enterprises in mainland China realized that              眾多國內龍頭企業也意識到合夥人制度與公司的
 the partnership systems is closely correlated to a sustainable        長遠發展是息息相關的，因此，紛紛推行合夥人
 development of the company, and start to implement the                管理模式，讓員工與公司形成利益共同體、事業
 partnership management system one after another, allowing             共同體、命運的共同體。
 employees and company to build a community of shared interests,
 business and future.

 The Employee Partnership Program is a model of contemporary           員工合夥人計畫是一種融合激勵與管理的模式，
 management that combines incentives and management.                   借助公司平台，以利益激勵合夥人，賦予其更多
 Leveraging the platform provided by the Company, the program          權利，並相應承擔更多為公司做貢獻義務。
 will motivate the partners on the basis of profit sharing while the
 partners will be empowered with more rights as well as to bear
 more responsibilities driving to the success of the Company.

 The management concept and model of “Employee Partnership             本 公 司 的「員 工 合 夥 人 計 畫」管 理 理 念 與 管 理 模
 Program“ includes two phases. The first phase is to implement the     式，首階段以「合夥人利潤分享」方式加以推行，
 “employee profit sharing“ program, and the second phase is to         第二階段按「盈利分享」與「業績進步獎勵」兩類方
 facilitate both “profit sharing“ and “performance progress reward”    案推行。隨著第一、二階的經驗積累，公司未來
 program. As accumulating experience during the implementation         繼續深入推動更全面、科學的員工合夥人計畫。
 of two phases, the Company will continue to launch a more
 comprehensive and scientific Employee Partnership Program in the
 future.

   Securities on The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited and

   each reporting period as explained in the accounting policies

   another valuation technique. In estimating the fair value             平值時，本集團考慮了市場參與者在計量日

   characteristics of the asset or liability if market participants      產或負債特徵。在該等綜合財務報表中計量
   would take those characteristics into account when pricing            及╱或披露的公平值均在此基礎上予以確

         measurements are categorised into Level 1, 2 or 3 based on              平值計量的輸入數據可觀察程度及輸入數據
         the degree to which the inputs to the fair value measurements           對公平值計量的整體重要性分類為第一級、
         are observable and the significance of the inputs to the fair           第二級及第三級，載述如下：
         value measurement in its entirety, which are described as
         follows:

         •       Level 1 inputs are quoted prices (unadjusted) in active         •       第一級輸入數據是實體於計量日期可
                 markets for identical assets or liabilities that the entity             以取得的相同資產或負債於活躍市場
                 can access at the measurement date;                                     的報價（未經調整）；

                 included within Level 1, that are observable for the asset              接或間接觀察的輸入數據（第一級內所
                 or liability, either directly or indirectly; and                        包含的報價除外）；及

         •       Level 3 inputs are unobservable inputs for the asset or         •       第三級輸入數據是資產或負債的不可
                 liability.                                                              觀察輸入數據。

         Basis of consolidation                                                  綜合基準
         The consolidated financial statements incorporate the                   綜合財務報表載有本公司以及由本公司及其
         financial statements of the Company and entities (including             附屬公司控制的實體（包括結構性實體）的財
         structured entities) controlled by the Company and its                  務報表。倘屬以下情況，則本公司獲得控制
         subsidiaries. Control is achieved when the Company:                     權：

         (i)     has power over the investee;                                    (i)     可對被投資者行使權力；

         (ii)    is exposed, or has rights, to variable returns from its         (ii)    因參與被投資者的業務而可獲得或有
                 involvement with the investee; and                                      權獲得可變回報；及

         (iii)   has the ability to use its power to affect its returns.         (iii)   有能力藉行使其權力而影響該等回
                                                                                         報。

         The Group reassesses whether or not it controls an investee             倘有事實或情況顯示上述三項控制因素中，
         if facts and circumstances indicate that there are changes to           有一項或以上出現變數，本集團會重新評估
         one or more of the three elements of control listed above.              其是否控制被投資者。

   Consolidation of a subsidiary begins when the Group obtains           本集團於獲得附屬公司控制權時將附屬公司
   control over the subsidiary and ceases when the Group loses           綜合入賬，並於失去附屬公司控制權時終止
   control of the subsidiary. Specially, income and expenses             入賬。具體而言，於年內購入或出售的附屬
   of a subsidiary acquired or disposed of during the year are           公司的收入及開支，自本集團獲得控制權當
   included in the consolidated statement of profit or loss and          日起至本集團失去附屬公司控制權當日止，
   other comprehensive income from the date the Group gains              計入綜合損益及其他全面收益表內。
   controls until the date when the Group ceases to control the
   subsidiary.

   Profit or loss and each component of other comprehensive              損益及各項其他全面收入項目歸屬於本公司
   income are attributed to the owners of the Company and to             擁有人及非控股權益。附屬公司的全面收入
   the non-controlling interests. Total comprehensive income of          總額歸屬於本公司擁有人及非控股權益，即
   subsidiaries is attributed to the owners of the Company and           使這將導致非控股權益出現虧絀結餘。
   to the non-controlling interests even if this results in the non-
   controlling interests having a deficit balance.

   statements of subsidiaries to bring their accounting policies         調整，使其會計政策與本集團的會計政策貫
   into line with the Group’s accounting policies.                       徹一致。

   the Group are eliminated in full on consolidation.                    流量已於綜合賬目時全額抵銷。

   separately from the Group’s equity therein, which represent           權益分開呈列，指現時擁有的權益且賦予持

   An associate is an entity over which the Group has significant      聯營公司為本集團擁有重大影響力的實體。

   incorporated in these consolidated financial statements using       計法計入該等綜合財務報表。權益會計法所

   like transactions and events in similar circumstances. Under        成本於綜合財務狀況表確認，並於其後就確

   of the associate. Changes in net assets of the associate other      本集團分佔聯營公司的虧損超出本集團於該
   than profit or loss and other comprehensive income are not          聯營公司的權益（包括實質上構成本集團於
   accounted for unless such changes resulted in changes in            聯營公司的投資淨額一部分的任何長期權
   ownership interest held by the Group. When the Group’s              益）時，本集團終止確認其分佔的進一步虧
   share of losses of an associate exceeds the Group’s interest        損。僅於本集團已產生法定或推定責任，或
   in that associate (which includes any long-term interests that,     已代表聯營公司支付款項的情況下，方會確

   the associate), the Group discontinues recognising its share

   date if the interest rate implicit in the lease is not readily

          less any lease incentives receivable;                                             應收租賃優惠；

   •      variable lease payments that depend on an index or a                          •   可 變 租 賃 付 款（視 乎 指 數 或 利 率 而
          rate; and                                                                         定）；及

          lease term reflects the Group exercising the option to                            則支付終止租賃的罰款。
          terminate.

         Variable lease payments that reflect changes in market rental       反映市場租金變動的可變租賃付款初步使用
         rates are initially measured using the market rental rates as       於開始日期的市場租金計量。不會依賴指數
         at the commencement date. Variable lease payments that              或利率的可變租賃付款不計入租賃負債及使
         do not depend on an index or a rate are not included in the         用權資產計量，並在觸發付款的事件或條件
         measurement of lease liabilities and right-of-use assets, and       出現的期間內確認為開支。
         are recognised as expense in the period on which the event
         or condition that triggers the payment occurs.

         After the commencement date, lease liabilities are adjusted         在開始日期後，租賃負債透過利息增加及租
         by interest accretion and lease payments.                           賃付款進行調整。

         The Group remeasures lease liabilities (and makes a                 於下列情況下，本集團重新計量租賃負債
         corresponding adjustment to the related right-of-use assets)       （並對相關的使用權資產進行相應調整）：
         whenever:

         •      the lease term has changed or there is a change in           •   租期有所變動或行使購買選擇權的評
                the assessment of exercise of a purchase option, in              估發生變化，在該情況下，相關租賃
                which case the related lease liability is remeasured by          負債透過使用重新評估當日經修訂貼
                discounting the revised lease payments using a revised           現率貼現經修訂租賃付款而重新計
                discount rate at the date of reassessment.                       量。

         •      the lease payments change due to changes in market           •   租賃付款因市場租金審查導致市場租
                rental rates following a market rent review, in which            賃利率變動而有所變化，在該情況
                cases the related lease liability is remeasured by               下，相關租賃負債透過使用初始貼現
                discounting the revised lease payments using the initial         率貼現經修訂租賃付款而重新計量。
                discount rate.

   aggregate stand-alone price of the non-lease components.

         bases used in the computation of taxable profit. Deferred tax             稅暫時差額確認。遞延稅項資產一般按可能
         liabilities are generally recognised for all taxable temporary            有應課稅溢利用作扣減可動用可扣減暫時差
         differences. Deferred tax assets are generally recognised for             額為限，就所有該等可扣減暫時差額確認。
         all deductible temporary differences to the extent that it is             倘若暫時差額由不影響應課稅溢利或會計溢
         probable that taxable profits will be available against which             利的交易中初次確認（業務合併除外）資產及
         those deductible temporary differences can be utilised. Such              負債所產生，則不會確認該等遞延稅項資產
         deferred tax assets and liabilities are not recognised if the             及負債。此外，倘暫時差額乃因初次確認商
         temporary difference arises from the initial recognition (other           譽而產生，亦不會確認遞延稅項負債。
         than in a business combination) of assets and liabilities in
         a transaction that affects neither the taxable profit nor the
         accounting profit. In addition, deferred tax liabilities are not
         recognised if the temporary difference arises from the initial
         recognition of goodwill.

         Deferred tax liabilities are recognised for taxable temporary             遞延稅項負債乃就與附屬公司及聯營公司投
         differences associated with investments in subsidiaries and               資有關的應課稅暫時差額確認，惟倘本集團
         associates, except where the Group is able to control the                 能夠控制暫時差額的撥回及暫時差額於可見
         reversal of the temporary difference and it is probable that              將來可能不會撥回則除外。
         the temporary difference will not reverse in the foreseeable
         future.

         The carrying amount of deferred tax assets is reviewed at the             遞延稅項資產的賬面值於報告期末進行檢
         end of the reporting period and reduced to the extent that                討，並扣減至不再有足夠應課稅溢利可供收
         it is no longer probable that sufficient taxable profit will be           回全部或部分資產。
         available to allow all or part of the asset to be recovered.

         Deferred tax liabilities and assets are measured at the tax               遞延稅項負債及資產按預期於結算負債或變
         rates that are expected to apply in the period in which the               現資產期間適用的稅率計量，所根據的稅
         liability is settled or the asset is realised, based on tax rate (and     率（及稅法）乃於報告期末已頒佈或已實際頒
         tax laws) that have been enacted or substantively enacted by              佈。
         the end of the reporting period.

         The measurement of deferred tax liabilities and assets reflects           遞延稅項負債及資產的計量反映本集團預期
         the tax consequences that would follow from the manner in                 於報告期末將要收回其資產或結算其負債賬
         which the Group expects, at the end of the reporting period,              面值的方式所產生的稅務後果。
         to recover or settle the carrying amount of its assets and
         liabilities.

   Financial instruments                                                     金融工具
   Financial assets and financial liabilities are recognised when a          金融資產及金融負債於一間集團實體成為工
   group entity becomes a party to the contractual provisions of             具合約條文訂約方時確認。所有以常規途徑
   the instrument. All regular way purchases or sales of financial           購入或銷售的金融資產乃按交易日期基準確
   assets are recognised and derecognised on a trade date                    認及取消確認。常規途徑購入或銷售指根據
   basis. Regular way purchases or sales are purchases or sales              市場法規或慣例所確定的時間框架內交付資
   of financial assets that require delivery of assets within the            產的金融資產購入或銷售。
   time frame established by regulation or convention in the
   market place.

   Financial assets and financial liabilities are initially measured         金融資產及金融負債初步按公平值計量，惟
   at fair value except for trade receivables arising from contracts         產生自與客戶合約的應收貿易賬款（初步按
   with customers which are initially measured in accordance                 國際財務報告準則第15號客戶合約收入（「國
   with IFRS 15 Revenue from Contracts with Customers (“IFRS                 際財務報告準則第15號」）計量）除外。收購
   15”). Transaction costs that are directly attributable to the             或發行金融資產及金融負債（按公平值計入
   acquisition or issue of financial assets and financial liabilities        損益（
                                                                               「按公平值計入損益」 ）的金融資產或金
   (other than financial assets or financial liabilities at fair value       融負債除外）直接應佔的交易成本於初步確
   through profit or loss (“FVTPL”)) are added to or deducted                認時於金融資產或金融負債（如適用）的公平
   from the fair value of the financial assets or financial liabilities,     值中加入或減去。收購按公平值計入損益的
   as appropriate, on initial recognition. Transaction costs                 金融資產或金融負債直接應佔的交易成本即
   directly attributable to the acquisition of financial assets or           時於損益中確認。
   financial liabilities at FVTPL are recognised immediately in
   profit or loss.

   The effective interest method is a method of calculating                  實際利率法乃計算金融資產或金融負債的攤
   the amortised cost of a financial asset or financial liability            銷成本以及於有關期間分配利息收入及利息
   and of allocating interest income and interest expense over               開支的方法。實際利率乃於初步確認時按金
   the relevant period. The effective interest rate is the rate              融資產或金融負債的預計可使用年期或（如
   that exactly discounts estimated future cash receipts and                 適用）較短期間內準確貼現估計未來現金收
   payments (including all fees and points paid or received that             入及款項（包括構成實際利率不可或缺部分
   form an integral part of the effective interest rate, transaction         的所有已付或已收費用及積分、交易成本及
   costs and other premiums or discounts) through the expected               其他溢價或貼現）至賬面淨值的利率。
   life of the financial asset or financial liability, or, where
   appropriate, a shorter period, to the net carrying amount on
   initial recognition.

         subsequently measured at amortised cost:                                量：

         •      the financial asset is held within a business model whose        •   於目的為收取合約現金流量的業務模
                objective is to collect contractual cash flows; and                  式下持有金融資產；及

         •      the contractual terms give rise on specified dates to            •   合約條款於特定日期產生的現金流量
                cash flows that are solely payments of principal and                 純粹為支付本金及尚未償還本金的利
                interest on the principal amount outstanding.                        息。

         subsequently measured at FVTOCI:                                        其他全面收入的方式計量：

         (iv) Write-off policy                                                 (iv) 撇銷政策
              The Group writes off a financial asset when there is                  資料顯示對手方處於嚴重財困及無實
              information indicating that the counterparty is in severe             際收回可能時，例如對手方被清盤或
              financial difficulty and there is no realistic prospect of            已進入破產程序時（以較早者為準），
              recovery, for example, when the counterparty has been                 本集團則撇銷金融資產。經考慮法律
              placed under liquidation or has entered into bankruptcy               意見後（倘合適），遭撇銷的金融資產
              proceedings, whichever occurs sooner. Financial assets                可能仍須按本集團收回程序進行強制
              written off may still be subject to enforcement activities            執行活動。撇銷構成取消確認事項。
              under the Group’s recovery procedures, taking into                    任何其後收回在損益中確認。
              account legal advice where appropriate. A write-off
              constitutes a derecognition event. Any subsequent
              recoveries are recognised in profit or loss.

         (v)    Measurement and recognition of ECL                             (v)   預期信貸虧損的計量及確認
                The measurement of ECL is a function of the probability              預期信貸虧損的計量取決於違約概
                of default, loss given default (i.e. the magnitude of the            率 、 違 約 損 失 率（即 違 約 時 的 虧 損 程
                loss if there is a default) and the exposure at default.             度）及違約風險承擔。違約概率及違約
                The assessment of the probability of default and loss                損失率的評估乃基於經前瞻性資料調
                given default is based on historical data adjusted by                整後的過往數據。預期信貸虧損的預
                forward-looking information. Estimation of ECL reflects              估乃無偏概率加權平均金額，以各自
                an unbiased and probability-weighted amount that is                  發生違約的風險為權重確定。
                determined with the respective risks of default occurring
                as the weights.

                Generally, the ECL is the difference between all                     一般而言，預期信貸虧損為根據合約
                contractual cash flows that are due to the Group in                  應付本集團的所有合約現金流量與本
                accordance with the contract and the cash flows that the             集團預期收取的現金流量（按於初始確
                Group expects to receive, discounted at the effective                認 時 釐 定 的 實 際 利 率 貼 現）之 間 的 差
                interest rate determined at initial recognition.                     額。

                Interest income is calculated based on the gross                     利息收入根據金融資產的賬面總值計
                carrying amount of the financial asset unless the                    算，惟金融資產為信貸減值的情況除
                financial asset is credit-impaired, in which case interest           外，在此情況下，利息收入按金融資
                income is calculated based on amortised cost of the                  產的攤銷成本計算。
                financial assets.

                The Group recognises an impairment gain or loss in                   本集團通過調整所有金融工具的賬面
                profit or loss for all financial instruments by adjusting            值於損益內確認其減值收益或虧損，
                their carrying amount, with the exception of trade and               惟相應調整於虧損撥備賬中確認的應
                other receivables where the corresponding adjustment                 收貿易賬款及其他應收款項除外。
                is recognised through a loss allowance account.

   In the application of the Group’s accounting policies, which         於 應 用 本 集 團 的 會 計 政 策（於 附 註 4論 述）

   apparent from other sources. The estimates and associated            其他因素作出。實際結果可能與該等估計有
   assumptions are based on historical experience and other             所不同。
   factors that are considered to be relevant. Actual results may
   differ from these estimates.

   The estimates and underlying assumptions are reviewed on             估計及相關假設會持續進行檢討。倘對會計
   an on-going basis. Revisions to accounting estimates are             估計的修訂僅影響對估計作出修訂的期間，
   recognised in the period in which the estimate is revised if         則於修訂有關估計的期間內確認，或倘修訂
   the revision affects only that period, or in the period of the       對當前及未來期間均有影響，則於修訂期間
   revision and future periods if the revision affects both current     及未來期間確認。
   and future periods.

   The tax charge represents income tax in the PRC which is            稅項開支指按於中國成立的附屬公司的應課
   calculated at the prevailing tax rate on the taxable income of      稅收入的現行稅率計算的中國所得稅。
   the subsidiaries established in the PRC.

   The PRC subsidiaries are subject to the PRC Enterprise              中國附屬公司須就該兩個年度按25%的稅率
   Income Tax (the “EIT Law”) at 25% for both years. Taxation          繳納中國企業所得稅（「企業所得稅法」）。其
   arising in other jurisdictions is calculated at the rates           他司法權區產生的稅項乃根據有關司法權區
   prevailing in the relevant jurisdictions. According to the          現行稅率計算。根據中國現行稅務規則及法
   prevailing tax rules and regulation of the PRC, 16 subsidiaries     規，截至二零一九年十二月三十一日止年
   (2018: 16 subsidiaries) of the Group are exempted from              度，本集團的16間附屬公司（二零一八年：
   enterprise income tax for taxable profit from the operation         16間附屬公司）於中國經營農業業務產生的
   of agricultural business in the PRC for the year ended 31           應課稅溢利獲豁免繳納企業所得稅。
   December 2019.

   No provision for taxation in Hong Kong has been made as the         並無就香港稅項作出撥備，原因為本集團於
   Group’s income neither arises in, nor is derived from Hong          香港並無產生收入亦無源自香港之收入。
   Kong.

   Pursuant to the PRC EIT Law, a 10% withholding tax is levied        根據中國企業所得稅法，於中國成立的外資
   on dividends declared to foreign investors from the foreign         企業向外商投資者宣派股息須繳納10%的預
   investment enterprises established in Mainland China. The           扣稅。該規定自二零零八年一月一日起生
   requirement is effective from 1 January 2008 and applies            效，並適用於二零零七年十二月三十一日後
   to earnings after 31 December 2007. A lower withholding             的盈利。倘中國與外商投資者所在司法權區
   tax rate may be applied if there is a tax treaty between            訂立了稅項條約，則可調低預扣稅稅率。
   China and the jurisdiction of the foreign investors. For the        本集團的適用比率為5%或10%。並未就中
   Group, the applicable rate is 5% or 10%. Deferred taxation          國附屬公司累計溢利人民幣1,863,415,000
   has not been provided for in the consolidated financial             元（二 零 一 八 年 十 二 月 三 十 一 日 ︰ 人 民 幣
   statements in respect of the accumulated profits of the PRC         1,372,185,000元）於綜合財務報表就遞延稅
   subsidiaries amounting RMB1,863,415,000 (31 December                項作出撥備，乃由於本集團能夠控制撥回暫
   2018: RMB1,372,185,000) as the Group is able to control the         時差額的時間及該等暫時差額於可見將來可

         Right-of-use assets are depreciated on a straight-line basis                 使用權資產按租期以直線法折舊。
         over the lease terms.


         The Group leases lands, properties, machineries and                          本集團租賃土地、物業、機械及設備以經
         equipments to operate its business. These leases are                         營其業務。該等租賃通常固定期限為2至50
         typically made for fixed terms of 2 to 50 years. Lease terms                 年。租賃條款乃按個別基準磋商，包含不同
         are negotiated on an individual basis and contain different                  付款條款及條件。租賃負債按未支付的租賃
         payment terms and conditions. The lease liabilities are                      付款現值計量。於二零一九年十二月三十一
         measured at the present value of the lease payments that are                 日，相關集團實體就租賃負債所應用增量借
         not yet paid. The incremental borrowing rates applied by the                 款利率介乎每年5.13%至5.20%。
         relevant group entities range from 5.13% to 5.20% per annum
         for lease liabilities as at 31 December 2019.


         For some of the leased machineries, the Group is required                    對於部分租賃機械，本集團須作出每月固定
         to make fixed monthly payments and additional variable                       付款及視乎合約期內使用資產情況作出額外
         payments depending on the usage of the asset during the                      可變付款。
         contract period.


         The Group’s lease agreements do not contain any contingent                   本集團的租賃協議不包含任何或然租金或承
         rent nor any extension, early termination option or purchase                 租人的任何延長、提早終止權或購買權。
         option for lessee.

         machineries, motor vehicles and office equipment. As at 31                   期租賃。於二零一九年十二月三十一日，短
         December 2019, the portfolio of short-term leases is similar                 期租賃組合與短期租賃開支於附註8所披露

         produce raw milk.                                                   牛。

         reporting period is shown below. The Group’s milkable cows          本集團的奶牛是養來產奶的乳牛。小母牛及
         are dairy cows held for milk production. Heifers and calves         小牛都是未滿產奶年齡的乳牛。
         are those dairy cows that have not reached the age that can

         The Group is exposed to fair value risks arising from changes       本集團要承受原料奶價格變動帶來的公平值
         in price of raw milk. The Group does not anticipate that            風險。據本集團預計，在可預見的將來原料
         the price of the raw milk will decline significantly in the         奶價格不會大幅下跌。董事認為，本集團並
         foreseeable future and the Directors are of the view that there     無可用的衍生工具或其他可以訂立的合約用
         is no available derivative or other contracts which the Group       於管理原料奶價格下跌的風險。
         can enter into to manage the risk of a decline in the price of
         the raw milk.

         In general, the heifers are inseminated with semen when             一般情況下，小母牛於長至約十四個月大時
         heifers reached approximately 14 months old. After an               進行配種。約九個月的孕期過後，小牛出

         and the dairy cow begins to produce raw milk and the                牛通常會擠奶約340天，之後進入約60天的
         lactation period begins. A milkable cow is typically milked         乾乳期。
         for approximately 340 days before approximately 60 days dry
         period.

         When a heifer begins to produce raw milk, it is transferred         小母牛開始產原料奶時，乃按轉撥日期的估
         to the category of milkable cows based on the estimated fair        計公平值轉撥至奶牛類別。
         value on the date of transfer.

39. CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                            39. 資本風險管理
   The Group manages its capital to ensure that entities in              本集團的資金管理旨在確保本集團旗下實體
   the Group will be able to continue as a going concern                 的持續經營能力，同時透過優化債務及股本
   while maximising the return to stakeholders through the               結餘為利益相關者謀求最大回報。本集團的
   optimisation of the debt and equity balance. The Group’s              整體策略較去年保持不變。
   overall strategy remains unchanged from prior year.

   The capital structure of the Group consists of net debt, which        本集團的資本架構由債務淨額（包括銀行借
   includes bank borrowings, medium-term notes, corporate                款、中期票據、公司債券及其他借款（分別
   bonds and other borrowings as disclosed in Notes 29, 30, 31           披露於附註29、30、31及32），扣除銀行結
   and 32 respectively, net of bank balances and cash and equity         餘及現金）及本公司擁有人應佔權益（包括已
   attributable to owners of the Company, comprising issued              發行股本、股份溢價及儲備）組成。
   share capital, share premium and reserves.

   The Group’s management reviews the capital structure                  本集團的管理層定期檢討資本架構。作為檢
   on a regular basis. As part of the review, the management             討的一環，管理層會考慮資本成本及各類資
   considers the cost of capital and the risks associated with           本涉及的風險。本集團透過發行新股份及發
   each class of capital. The Group will balance its overall capital     行新債務或贖回現有債務等方式保持整體資
   structure through the issues of new shares and the issue of           本架構的平衡。
   new debt or the redemption of existing debt.

   (ii) Interest rate risk                                               (ii) 利率風險
        The Group is exposed to cash flow interest rate risk due              本集團因銀行結餘、已質押銀行結
        to fluctuations in the prevailing market interest rates on            餘、未質押銀行存款及按浮動利率計
        bank balances, pledged bank balances, non-pledged                     息銀行借款的現行市場利率波動而承
        bank deposits and bank borrowings which carry interest                受現金流量利率風險。
        at variable interest rates.

         The Group’s fair value interest rate risk relates primarily        本集團的公平值利率風險主要涉及定
         to fixed-rate bank and other borrowings, medium-term               息銀行及其他借款、中期票據、公司
         notes, corporate bonds and lease liabilities.                      債券及租賃負債。

         The Group manages its interest rate risk by performing             本集團通過定期審查利率風險淨額，
         regular reviews of the Group’s net interest rate                   並可能訂立利率掉期合約（如需要），
         exposures and may enter into interest rate swap                    以管理其利率風險。年內，本集團已
         contracts, when necessary, to manage its interest rate             訂立七份利率掉期合約以減輕其現金
         exposure. During the current year, seven interest rate             流量利率風險（二零一八年：三份）。
         swap contracts had been entered by the Group to
         mitigate its cash flow interest rate risk (2018: three).

         The sensitivity analyses below have been determined                以下敏感度分析乃根據非衍生工具於
         based on the exposure to interest rates for non-                   報告期末的利率風險釐定。以下敏感
         derivative instruments at the end of the reporting                 度分析乃根據與利率掉期合約無關的
         period. The sensitivity analyses below have been                   浮息銀行借款的利率風險釐定。由於
         determined based on the exposure to interest rates                 銀行結餘、已質押銀行結餘及未質押
         for variable interest rate bank borrowings that are not            銀行存款對利率波動並不敏感，故不
         associated to an interest rate swap contract. Bank                 在敏感度分析的考慮範圍內。該分析
         balances, pledged bank balances and non-pledged                    乃根據假設浮息銀行借款於整個年度
         bank deposits are excluded from the sensitivity analyses           未 償 還 而 編 製 。 50個 基 點（二 零 一 八

         As at 31 December 2019, the Group’s maximum exposure to                於二零一九年十二月三十一日，本集團面臨
         credit risk which will cause a financial loss to the Group due         因對手方未能履行義務而給本集團造成經濟
         to failure to discharge an obligation by the counterparties is         損失的最大信貸風險來自綜合財務狀況表所
         arising from the carrying amount of the respective recognised          列的各項已確認金融資產的賬面值。本集團
         financial assets as stated in the consolidated statement of            並無持有任何抵押品或其他信貸增強措施以
         financial position. The Group does not hold any collateral or          為其金融資產相關信貸風險提供保障，惟與
         other credit enhancements to cover its credit risks associated         自分銷商應收貿易賬款相關的信貸風險而減
         with its financial assets, except that the credit risks associated     輕除外，因該等款項於二零一八年十二月三
         with trade receivables from the Distributors is mitigated              十一日由在英屬處女群島註冊成立的五間第
         because they are secured over ordinary shares of the                   三方公司持有的本公司普通股作抵押。
         Company held by five third-party companies incorporated in
         British Virgin Islands as at 31 December 2018.

         Trade receivables arising from contracts with customers                客戶合約產生的應收貿易賬款
         In order to minimise the credit risk, the management of the            為盡量降低信貸風險，本集團管理層已指定
         Group has delegated a team responsible for determination of            團隊負責決定信貸額度及信貸批核。接納任
         credit limits and credit approvals. Before accepting any new           何新客戶前，本集團利用內部信貸評分系統
         customer, the Group uses an internal credit scoring system             對潛在客戶的信貸質素進行評估並且釐定其
         to assess the potential customer’s credit quality and defines          信貸額度。客戶應佔的限額與評級每年審閱
         credit limits by customer. Limits and scoring attributed to            兩次。已制定其他監察程序，以確保採取跟
         customers are reviewed twice a year. Other monitoring                  進行動收回逾期債務。此外，本集團在應用
         procedures are in place to ensure that follow-up action is             國際財務報告準則第9號後按預期信貸虧損
         taken to recover overdue debts. In addition, the Group                 模式個別對貿易結餘進行減值評估。就此而
         performs impairment assessment under ECL model upon                    言，董事認為本集團的信貸風險已大幅減

         regard, the Directors consider that the Group’s credit risk is

  42. FAIR VALUE MEASUREMENTS                                                 42. 公平值計量
         Fair value of the Group’s biological assets, financial                 按經常性基準以公平值或公平值減銷售
         assets and financial liabilities that are measured at fair             成本計量的本集團生物資產、金融資產
         value or fair value less cost to sell on a recurring basis             及金融負債的公平值
         The Group’s biological assets are measured at fair value less          本集團的生物資產按公平值減出售成本計
         costs to sell, financial assets at FVTPL, equity investment at         量，按公平值計入損益的金融資產、按公平
         FVTOCI and derivative financial instruments are measured at            值計入其他全面收入的權益投資及衍生金融
         fair value at the end of each reporting period. The Directors          工具於各報告期末按公平值計量。董事已成
         have set up a valuation team, which is headed up by the                立估值團隊，由本公司財務總監領導，以釐
         chief financial officer of the Company, to determine the               定適當的估值技術及公平值計量的輸入數
         appropriate valuation techniques and inputs for fair value             據。
         measurements.

         In estimating the fair value of an asset or a liability, the Group     估計資產或負債的公平值時，本集團使用可
         uses market-observable data to the extent it is available.             用市場可觀察數據。倘第一級及第二級的輸
         Where Level 1 and Level 2 inputs are not available, the Group          入數據不可用，本集團會委聘第三方合資格
         engages third party qualified valuers to perform the valuation.        估值師進行估值。估值團隊與合資格外聘估
         The valuation team works closely with the qualified external           值師緊密合作，為模型確立合適的估值方法
         valuers to establish the appropriate valuation techniques and          及輸入數據。首席財務官每半年向董事呈報
         inputs to the model. The chief financial officer reports the           估值團隊的發現以闡釋資產及負債公平值波
         valuation team’s findings to the Directors semi-annually to            動的原因。
         explain the cause of fluctuations in the fair value of the assets
         and liabilities.

                                     貼現現金流量未來現金流量乃根據遠期
                                       利率（來自報告期末的可觀察利率）及合約
                                       利率估計，按反映多名對手方的信貸
                                       風險的利率貼現。
